5a1(1): ci bcom ldan 'das rgyang ring po nas gshegs pa mthong ngo //mthong nas kyi rings par rangs par 'das kyi ched du bshams 
5a2(1): nas|'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das chur gshegs/bcom ldan 'das gshegs pa lags so//bcom ldan 'das gdan bshams pa 
5a3(1): la bzhugs su gsol//bcom ldan 'das bzhugs so//de nas rigs kyi bu dga' bo bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis 'jug pa tshal te/bcom ldan 
5a4(1): 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/rigs kyi bu dga' bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//dga' bo ci'i phyir rab tu mi byung 
5a5(1): dga' bos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i tshig gi don ci lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/sdig pa thams cad 
5a6(1): yongs su spong ba yin no//'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug rab tu sel ba yin no//'thab pa dang /'thab mo dang /'tshang 'dru ba dang /rtsod pa 
5a7(1): dang|'gyed pa dang /sgyu dang /g.yo'i mun pas bsdus pa'i dri ma thams cad 'khrud pa yin no//lo tog thams cad kyis gzhi lta bur yon tan thams 
5a8(1): cad kyi gzhi yin no//rin po che thams cad kyi 'byung gnas yin no//bde ba thams cad kyi yid bzhin gyi nor bu yin no//yon tan thams cad kyi 
5b1(1): go 'phang gi gnas yin no//sems can thams cad 'bugs 'byin pa yin no//'byung po thams cad kyi yid brtan pa yin no//srog chags thams cad 
5b2(1): la mi 'jigs pa sbyin pa yin no//sems can dbul po thams cad kyi bum pa yin no//skya bas med pa rnams kyi dpung gnyen yin no//kun tu 'tsho bas 
5b3(1): 'phongs pa dang/sdug bsngal gyi nyams thag pa rnams kyi bsti sa yin no//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mi 'dres pa yin no//sems can thabs 
5b4(1): cad kyi mchod par bya ba yin no//mkhas pa rnams kyis bsngags pa yin no//thar par 'gro ba rnams kyi lam yin no//sems can dag pa rnams 
5b5(1): kyi bsnyen par bya ba yin no//snod du gyur pa rnams kyi bstan par bya ba yin no//ngan song gi sgo gcod pa yin no//mtho ris dang thar pa'i sgo 'byed pa yin 
5b6(1): no||lha'i 'gro ba rnams kun tu 'gengs pa yin no//sdug bsngal thams cad kun tu spyod pa yin no//bsred pa'i bcing bas bcings pa rnams 
5b7(1): rnam par grol bar 'byed pa yin te/dga' bo de ni rab tu byung ba zhes bya'o//yang dag pa'i lta ba'i khyim yin no//thar pa'i yang dag par 
5b8(1): sdom pa yin no//mya ngan las 'das pa la bsnyen pa yin no//rgyun nye bar gcod pa yin no//nyon mongs pa sreg pa yin no//phra rgyas pa rnams 
6a1(1): 'byin pa yin no//lta ba ngan pa 'dri ba rnams 'jig par byed pa yin no//bdud kyi rgyal mtshan snyol ba yin no//bzod pa smra ba bsgreng ba yin 
6a2(1): no||'khor ba'i 'khor lo ldog par byed do//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar byed pa yin no//nu zhu'i rgya mtsho skyems pa yin no//rus pa'i ri 'jig par byed pa 
6a3(1): yin no//skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /'chi ba'i mtha' yin no//phung po'i khu 'jog pa yin no//nyon mongs pa'i 'dam du bying ba rnams 'byin pa 
6a4(1): yin no//khyim gyi gze ba du bcings pa'i rnams rnam par grol bar byed pa yin no//gnod pa'i tshor ba rnams zad par byed pa yin no//the tshom rnams dang /
6a5(1): nyon mongs pa'i dri ma brtan por gyur pa rnams gcod par yin no//rga shis 'jigs pa rnams dbugs 'byin par byed pa yin no//sdug bsngal dang 
6a6(1): bral ba zad par byed pa yin no//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal gyis mdzes pa rnams kyi bde ba dang /mi 'jigs pa'i gram yin no//'dod pa'i khams 
6a7(1): las rgal bar byed pa yin no/gzugs kyi khams las yang dag par rgal bar byed pa yin no//gzugs kyi khams las rnam par rgal bar byed pa 
6a8(1): yin no||don dam par mya ngan las 'das pa mchog thob par byed pa yin te/dga' bo de ni rab tu byung ba zhes bya'o//'jig rten thams cad kyi mig 
6b1(1): lta bu yin no//gti mug gi mun pa'i sgon me yin no//rgyud dag par byed pa yin no//nyon mongs pa'i nad thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa yin no//lam 
6b2(1): stor ba rnams la lam kun tu ston pa yin no//chos thams cad kyi de kho na ston pa yin no//mdud pa thams cad rnam par grol bar byed pa yin no//ma 
6b3(1): rig pa'i mun pa thams cad sel ba yin no//mi shes pa'i ra ba rib kyi sgrib pa'i mun pa rnams 'drel bar byed pa yin no/le lo can rnams brtson 'grus 
6b4(1): la rtsom par byed pa yin no/tshul khrims la ltung ba rnams skye bar byed pa yin no/tshul khrams phun sum tshogs pa rnams rab tu dga' bar byed pa yin 
6b5(1): no||brtson 'grus brtsams pa rnams mchog tu dga' ba yin no//nad du yang dag par 'jog la mngon par dga' ba'i yin no//rnal 'byor spyod 
6b6(1): pa rnams mchog tu cho ga shes par byed pa yin no//nyon mongs pa'i dgra 'joms par byed pa rnams kyi mtshon cha yin no//phyir mi 'ong bar rnams mchog 
6b7(1): tu rgyal bar byed pa yin no/gnas med pa rnams kyi gnas yin no//skyob pa rnams rnams kyi skyob pa yin no//'jigs pa las 'brel pa rnams kyi mi   
6b8(1): 'jigs par byed pa yin no/skyabs pa med pa rnams kyi skyabs yin no//sdug bsngal rnams kyi bde ba'i yo byad yin no//'khor ba'i bcing 
7a1(1): @#//bas bcings pa rnams grol bar byed pa yin no//dga' bo de ni rab tu byung ba zhes bya'o//lha dang mi rnams kyi bkur sdi byas pa 
7a2(1): yin no//mi'i dbang po rnams kyis mchod pa yin no//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa yin no//khyim gyi gnas yongs su gtong ba yin no//mya ngan 
7a3(1): las 'das pa'i lam 'dren pa'i yin no//skra'i khur 'dreg pa yin no//gos dur smrig ni 'dod chags dang bral ba'i rtags yin no//rig pa la brtson 
7a4(1): 'grus kyi zhing yin no//'di ni drang srong rnams kyi rgyal mtshan yin no//bdud sdig rto can rnams kyi 'dregs pa 'joms pa yin no//lha dang mir rnams rab tu dga' 
7a5(1): bar byed pa yin no//dka' bo rab tu byung ba ni de lta bu yin no//ci'i phyir rab tu mi 'byung/dga' bo rab tu byung ba ni 'khor lo sgyur ba'i rgyal srid kyi bde ba  
7a6(1): bas kyang ches khyad par du 'phags par bde ba dang yin no//dga' bo gzhan yang dal ba tshogs pa na bskal pa bye ba brgyar yang shin tu rnyed par dka' ba yin 
7a7(1): te|sangs rgyas 'byung ba ni de bas kyang ches rnyed par dka' ba yin no//dga' bo thar par 'dod pa rgyal po'i drang srong rnams dang/gzhan yang 'khor los 
7a8(1): sgyur ba'i rgyal po rin po che sna bdun dang ldan pa rnams kyang/btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas te bor nas rab tu byung ngo//de bzhin du khyim bdag dang /bram ze'i 
7b1(1): tshogs gzhan rnams kyang khyim rnams bor nas rab tu byung ngo//de nas bcom ldan 'das la rigs kyi bu dga' bos 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun 
7b2(1): pa bdag ni khyim par mchis la bdag ni sbyin pa dag bstsal pa/bsod nams dag bgyi zhing/bcom ldan 'das nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa la 
7b3(1): yang bsnyen bkur bgyi zhing/chos gos dang/bsod snyoms dang/gzims cha dang/snyun gyi gsos sman dang/yo byad rnams kyang sgrub par bgyi'o/
7b4(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis khams gsum pa

7b6(1): snyoms dang/mal stan dang/nad kyi gso sman dang/yo byad rnams kyis bsnyen bkur byed na/dga' bo de ni rab tu byang bar nges par 'byung ba'i 
7b7(1): sems kyi bcu drug gi cher mi phod do//dga' bo khyod kyis rab tu byung/dal ba shin tu rnyed dka' ste/dga' bo ston pa mngon gyur pa/nga la mi mnyes ma 
7b8(1): byed cigdga' bo dper na sa chen po 'di chu gcig pur rgya mtshor gyur la de na rus spal zhar ba gcig kyang yod/de na gnya' zhig shing gi bu ga gcig pa 
9a1(1): @#//gi phru ga gu'i rna mchog dang/sprul gyi lce dang/zil pa dang/rgya mtsho bskyod pa'i rla bas ltar g.yo ba'i yid/skad cig re re la yang mi sdod par 
9a2(1): 'gro ba/rtse gcig pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi stobs kyis yud tsam zhig rgyu ba nang du dpag tu gsol//smras pa/ri gzar 'bab pa'i rba brlabs gyur pas kyang//gang 
9a3(1): gi rba rlabs 'gyur la bsgrun med par/'jigs pa thogs med g.yo ba'i yid de ni//yud tsam rtse gcig mdzod cig btsun pa rnams/lha'i bu mo yi chad shugs  
9a4(1): phyung skad cig thi mir byas pa'i mig/ring zhig gzi ldan bkra la 'od 'tsher mig chags bal ba g.yo ba yis//gang gi thugs ni skye ba rgyun gcod rgyu las 'khrugs par 
9a5(1): ma gyur pa//bdud kyang 'joms mkhas gcig pu thams cad mkhyen pa de la phyag mdzod cig/skye ldan gang du brten nas dud 'gro'i skye gnas sems can dmyal bzhi dang /
9a6(1): yi dgas 'jig rten gnas su 'gro bar mi 'gyur  sdug bsngal ('jig )mi byed pa/'khor ba dag na 'gro ba chen po rnams kyi grogs gyur 'phags gcig po//zhi ba de grong 
9a7(1): khyer sgor gzhol de la gus pas bsten par mdzod//zhi ba'i bdag nyid gang zhig bde gshegs gsung rab mthar phyin 'byung smra zhing//gang la gus par cung zad tsam zhig 
9a8(1): byin yang sbyin pa 'phel gyur la/ye shes snang bas mun pa zad byas dge slong dge 'dun de la ni/nyon mongs bcad pa'i tshad du thams cad kyis kyang gus par phyag mdzod cig/
9b1(1): lang lo chun po'i sgra mang ma go la thogs shing dpung ma go gnyis g.yog la/rang gi 'od zer phyogs mthar 'phro ba rnams kyis 'jig rten kun 'gengs pa//lha dbang rgya byin 
9b2(1): cod pan btud de tshangs la phyag byas gang yin te/phyi nas dga' byas sems kyi ting 'dzin shin tu chen po'i 'bras bu yin//rdo rje 'od 'phro phrang bas dpung rgyan 'dis 
9b3(1): nas mdzes gyur cing //lha la dka' stsol dbang po mtho ris skyong ba gang yin pa//de dag dge ba dag 'byung rnam smin 'gyur dus nyams dga' zhing //
9b4(1): mngon par 'dod pa'i 'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa'i rgyu rnams yin/kha nas rgyags 'gyur dri zhim 'byung zhing mig mi mtha' dma' la//dbugs 'byung shal 
9b5(1): g.yos pas nu ma 'bur la 'drung bar byed//sdug gu lhan cig nor bdag 'ga' tshal gdug pa gang yin pa de yang //'gro ba nyams su myong ba dga' sbyin pa 
9b6(1): yi bdag nyid chen po yin//'brug sgra sgrog cing sprin rnams chu ni kun tu len byed pa//rlabs 'byung dus na nor bu byi ru dung dang nya phyis 'byung //rin chen gnas ni 
9b7(1): chu lha nyid kyis 'byor pas gang srung ba//der yang rgyu ni sbyin pas mchog cing thub pas bka' stsal to//gang yang 'od zer rnams kyis mkha' la skar ma'i 'khor 
9b8(1): lo zil gnan cing //ku mu ta'i rdzing yangs rnams su dpal ni rna phye nas//glang po'i mche ba'i dpal dang ldan cig zhing mtshan mo shin tu ra ba mdzes pa//de yang 
10a1(1): @#//nges par sbyin pa nyid kyi 'bras bu ro bro mang po yin//ri rab rtse la shing rtas lam khar 'dug pa'i rin chen rdo gcog cing//rta'i rkang lag 
10a2(1): rnams kyis dgu tshigs chu bo rlabs ni gcod byed la//mun pa gsal phyir nyi mas skar ma zil mnan lham me gang yin pa//de rgyu yid kyis ting nge 'dzin gyis dag pa'i sbyin 
10a3(1): par thub pas gsungs//rgyal mtshan rtser btags rin chen 'od zer snang bas nam mkha' dmar gyur cing//rta mig 'dor bas mkha' la sprin gyi phreng ba dag kyang 'dren 
10a4(1): byed la||rnga ya ba dkar pos g.yabs shing 'khor los bsgyur ba song ba gang yin pa//'dod pa'i 'bras bu de yang sbyin pas bsgra bas zhes thub pas bka' 
10a5(1): stsal to//gser gyi pad+ma'i rdul gyi phye mas dmar bris tshon ni khrus byas te//de'i na gas 'dab bung ba sgra 'byin rnams su nyal ba'i bde rnyed nas//rigs 
10a6(1): 'dzin chung mas lhan cig 'jigs med mkha' las 'gro ba gang yin pa//de yang 'jil zhing skyon med dga' ba'i sbyin pa'i gzi brjid yin//ma la ya'i ri rtse tsan 
10a7(1): dan gri ba mas bsil bas yangs pa dang//'bigs byed ri dang gangs dang lhun po'i ri dang man+da ra'i ri bo la//grub pa'i tshogs chen chung ba lhan cig 'jig la dga' 
10a8(1): ba gang yin pa//'dod ba'i 'bras bu tshe 'di'i snying po 'byor pa de yang gtong ba yin//bud med rkang gdub 'khrul pas 'drogs nas 'phur pa'i phug ron gnas pa yi/
10b1(1): khang pa mthon po rnams dang 'bigs byed ri rtse 'gron pa'i bal glang dang//gdugs dang rnga yab bud med g.yog 'khor rin chen gser dang rta rnams dang //bzang 
10b2(1): mdzes de dag kun kyang sngon gyi sbyin pa'i dge ba'i rnam smin yin//ban du ka ltar tsa dmar dgun chang gser snon bzang por legs blugs pa//yongs dag 
10b3(1): a mra'i smin ltar dri zhim ud pal 'gyid zhing ldem//na chung zhen pa mna' sgo gdug kyis stobs shing yams grangs mnga' grim pa//mchog tu dge ba de rgyu smin

10b6(1): zas gtsang sras pos gsungs//de lta legs pas bde bar gshegs pa'i gsung rab mtha' dag thugs chud pa//shin tu rgyas pa'i chu ltar gyur pa snying po dri ma med pa dang/
10b7(1): lha'i skya bo dang klu'i gnas su gyur pa//tshul khrims 'dren pa ma yin zhing rgya che ba'i chu dang ldan pa dge ba la rnam par rtog pa'i zla ba shar ba'i dbang gis//ting nge 'dzin 
10b8(1): sna tshogs rgyas pa'i dus kyi rlabs kyis byang chub kyi yan lag skyon med pa'i nor bu mang po kun tu g.yangs pa//sems kyi 'o ga gzhi la 'phags pa'i lam yan lag 
11a1(1): @#//brgyad pa'i ri bo chen po brten pa /'bras bu'i bye brag thob pa/gang zag chen po'i chu sprin rgyu ba/'phags pa'i bden pa dang/yang dag par 
11a2(1): spong ba dang/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa'i shing ljon pas mdzes par byas pa/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa'i dogs dang ldan pa/sgrib pa'i ro mi gnas pa/sbyangs pa'i yon tan   
11a3(1): goms pa'i dag pa'i mu tig gi nya phyis kyi khog lus dang ldan pa/mngon par rtogs pa'i chos nyid gting mi dpogs shing zab pa/rgyun mi 'chad par klog pa dang/kha 
11a4(1): ton byed pa'i sgra rgya cher 'byung ba/'phags pa'i dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi rgya mtsho chen po la bdag gsol ba 'tshal lo//'di lta ste srid pa'i 'gro ba gcig nas gcig 
11a5(1): tu rgyud pa dum nas dge ba'i sa bon btab pa/bde bar gshegs pa'i bstan pa/gzhal med khang gis bzhi gcig par gyur pa/bzod pa dang/nges pa dang/
11a6(1): byams pa dang/tshul khrims dang/thos pa dang/zhi ba'i gnas/bdag dang gzhan gyi gzhung don gyi de kho na ma lus par rtogs pa/yongs su shes pas bsgoms pas 
11a7(1): dran pa nye bar gnas pa/yang dag pa'i ye shes mthong bas log par lta ba'i rab rib bsal ba/legs par brjod cing shis la rgya che ba'i mi dang ldan pa'i skye bo bla ma la bsnyen 
11a8(1): bkur byed pa dang/mngon du smra ba ('i)dang/phyag 'tshal ba lasogs pa'i tshul bzang po dang ldan zhing mdong gsol che bas snyen pa rnams bskyed pa/ston gyi zla ba nya ba'i 'do

11b2(1): 'khor gyi bla dang /mu shil de nga/ta mu sa dang /go shil dang /sta ba kar ni sa dang/mgon med zas sbyin dang/drag po dang /nag po dang/nam gru la sogs pa dang/gzhan yang

11b7(1): ngan pas rnyed par dka' ba ser sna can gyi yid 'jom par bsgrub pa na/ston gyi zla ba dri ma med pa ltar/dang ba'i thugs mnga' ba/srid pa'i nyon mongs pa'i sa bon 
11b8(1): gcod pa/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa mkhyen pa/gra gas pa'i 'jig rten gsum du khyab pa/bdud kyi bu mo steg pa rgyun gcod pa'i rgyu/skyabs su 'os

12a6(1): kyi dri bas phyogs kyi dkyil 'khor ma lus par bkra bar byas pas/kha na ma tho ba med par zhugs pa /'phags pa'i bden pa ston pa/zhi bar gzhol 
12a7(1): ba'i lam du 'dren pa/'jig rten sgrol ba mnyes gshin pa /gnyen gcig pu/mu stegs can ngan pa'i rgol ba rnams kyis gsung dang/thugs dang/
12a8(1): dpa' ba dang/mthu la mi gnod pa gtsang ma'i sras yul rnams kun la sred pa 'byung brgyun skams byed pa'i rgyu/skye bo mkhas pa rnams ni sad par bya phyir shar 
12b1(1): gyur pa;bag la nyal gyi rab rib bsal ba ye shes snang ba'i mchog /sa rnams mtha' dag snang ba phyogs rnams thams cad snang mdzad pa//phyag dang zhabs dang 
12b2(1): dbu ni rab tu byin zhing tshul khrims dang//smon lam dag gis byang chub lam bzang yang dag bsgrubs gyur pa//srid pa'i 'gro ba'i dug gis snye ma'i snyed du lhung ba yi//
12b3(1): skye dgu rnams la kha na ma tho med pa ston mdzad pa//srid pa rnam pa gsum du 'jig pa/bcom ldan 'das stobs bcu'i stobs dang ldan pa/mi 'jigs pa bzhi 
12b4(1): mnga' ba/dpa' ba dang/mthu kha na ma tho ba med pa dang/mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos brgyan pas sku brjid pa/pha rol tu phyin pa drug dang/dka' ba brgya

12b8(1): ra ba dang/ri dgas nags 'dab dang/khrad khrod dang/chu dang ri sul dang/gsher dang ne'u sing dang/shing gi sgrib ma rnam pa sna tshogs la dgyes pa yul dang/ljongs du
